The 4 agents run concurrently and each one's completion arrives as a message in this conversation when it finishes — do NOT issue a separate blocking call to wait for them.\n285\t\n286\t**Once all 4 agents have reported completion, read each agent's output file (single message with 4 Read calls):**\n287\t```\n288\tRead tool:\n289\t  file_path: \"{outputFile from that agent's async_launched result}\"\n290\t```\n291\t\n292\t> Allow up to `workflow.subagent_timeout` for the slowest agent to finish before treating it as failed. The timeout is configurable via `workflow.subagent_timeout` in `.planning/config.json` (milliseconds). Default: 300000 (5 minutes). Increase for large codebases or slower models.\n293\t\n294\tEach output file contains that agent's completion confirmation. Parse the confirmation marker (see below) from the file contents.\n295\t\n296\t**Expected confirmation format from each agent:**\n297\t```\n298\t## Mapping Complete\n299\t\n300\t**Focus:** {focus}\n301\t**Documents written:**\n302\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC1}.md` ({N} lines)\n303\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC2}.md` ({N} lines)\n304\t\n305\tReady for orchestrator summary.\n306\t```\n307\t\n308\t**What you receive:** Just file paths and line counts. NOT document contents.\n309\t\n310\tIf any agent failed, note the failure and continue with successful documents.\n311\t\n312\tContinue to verify_output.\n313\t</step>\n314\t\n315\t<step name=\"sequential_mapping\" condition=\"Agent tool is NOT available (e.g. Antigravity, Codex)\">\n316\tWhen the `Agent` tool is unavailable, perform codebase mapping sequentially in the current context. This replaces `spawn_agents` and `collect_confirmations`.\n317\t\n318\t**IMPORTANT:** Do NOT use `browser_subagent`, `Explore`, or any browser-based tool. It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. Review the flagged content above\n2. If these are real secrets, they must be removed before committing\n3. Reply \"safe to proceed\" if the flagged content is not actually sensitive, or edit the files first.\n```\n\nWait for user confirmation before continuing to commit_codebase_map.\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=false:**\n\nContinue to commit_codebase_map.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"commit_codebase_map\">\nCommit the codebase map:\n\n```bash\ngsd_run query commit \"docs: map existing codebase\" --files .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\nContinue to offer_next.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"offer_next\">\nPresent completion summary and next steps.\n\n**Get line counts:**\n```bash\nwc -l .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\n**Output format:**\n\n```\nCodebase mapping complete.\n\nCreated .planning/codebase/:\n- STACK.md ([N] lines) - {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0192vzXvnzWKS1MKYqDnSXxp","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Architecture\n2\t\n3\tStatus: Accepted\n4\t\n5\tThis document describes how the portfolio site is structured: layers, import rules, folder layout, data flow, the content model, the design-token pipeline and the animation layer. It is written so the scaffold can be built directly from it. Decisions with a longer rationale live in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n6\t\n7\t## Overview\n8\t\n9\tThe site is a static, zero-backend, single-page React app served from GitHub Pages. There is no router, no global store and no data fetching. The architecture comes down to four seams:\n10\t\n11\t1. Typed content: all copy lives in TypeScript modules checked against one schema.\n12\t2. CSS-variable design tokens: five role colors per theme drive every surface.\n13\t3. An internal animation layer: the only place the two animation libraries are imported.\n14\t4. A build and deploy pipeline: Vite build, GitHub Actions, GitHub Pages.\n15\t\n16\tEverything else is conventional React composition with props flowing down.\n17\t\n18\t## Layers\n19\t\n20\tDependencies point down only. A layer may import from layers below it, never above.\n21\t\n22\t```text\n23\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n24\t| app/        main.tsx, App.tsx, Providers, Page.tsx                    |\n25\t|             Page.tsx is the ONLY file that imports content data       |\n26\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n27\t| sections/   Hero, About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education,     |\n28\t|             Contact                      (props in, JSX out)          |\n29\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n30\t| layout/     Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav, Footer,          |\n31\t|             SkipLink, Wordmark                                        |\n32\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n33\t| components/ ui/ (CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons)                  |\n34\t|             motion-primitives/ (vendored source, treated as owned)    |\n35\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n36\t| anim/       the internal animation layer: the only place that         |\n37\t|             imports motion/react and @react-spring/web               |\n38\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n39\t| hooks/  lib/                  | styles/ (tokens.css, base.css,       |\n40\t| (useActiveSection, useTheme,  |  index.css = tailwind + theme map)   |\n41\t|  cn, a11y)                    |                                      |\n42\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n43\t| content/ (typed TS data)      assets/ (images, fonts via Fontsource) |\n44\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n45\tBuild and deploy: vite.config.ts, lint boundary rules, GitHub Actions\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\t## Import rules\n49\t\n50\tOnly src/anim/ and src/components/motion-primitives/ may import motion/react or @react-spring/web.\n51\t\n52\tOnly src/app/Page.tsx imports content data.\n53\t\n54\t| Importer | May import | May not import |\n55\t|----------|-----------|----------------|\n56\t| sections/* | layout, components/ui, the anim barrel (src/anim/index.ts), content types (not data) | motion/react, @react-spring/*, components/motion-primitives/* directly, content data |\n57\t| layout/*, components/ui/* | the anim barrel, lib, hooks | sections, content data |\n58\t| components/motion-primitives/* | motion/react, lib/cn | everything else (vendor code, kept out of lint autofix churn) |\n59\t| anim/* | motion/react, @react-spring/web, components/motion-primitives/*, the token mirror in styles | sections, layout |\n60\t| app/Page.tsx | content data, sections | any animation library or anim internals |\n61\t\n62\tThese rules are enforced by oxlint's `no-restricted-imports` rule with per-folder overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, covering dynamic `import()` and re-exports as well, so a violation fails CI rather than relying on review. Unit tests under `tests/unit` may import the libraries for assertions. A gesture library is not adopted up front. It would be added to the anim row only if a react-spring drag cannot be built well otherwise, after a React 19 compatibility check.\n63\t\n64\t## Folder structure\n65\t\n66\t```text\n67\trepo root\n68\t|-- public/                  URL-stable files only: favicon set, og image,\n69\t|                            resume.pdf (a phone-number-free export), robots.txt\n70\t|-- index.html               meta and social tags, inline theme-init script,\n71\t|                            noscript fallback content\n72\t|-- 404.html                 second HTML entry: the not-found page that\n73\t|                            GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths\n74\t|-- vite.config.ts           base from env, Tailwind plugin, imagetools, \"@\" alias\n75\t|-- .oxlintrc.json           import-boundary rules (see Import rules)\n76\t|-- .github/workflows/       ci.yml, deploy.yml\n77\t`-- src/\n78\t    |-- main.tsx\n79\t    |-- app/                 App.tsx, Page.tsx, Providers.tsx\n80\t    |-- sections/            one folder per section (Hero, About, Experience,\n81\t    |                        Projects, Skills, Education, Contact)\n82\t    |-- layout/              Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav,\n83\t    |                        Footer, SkipLink, Wordmark\n84\t    |-- components/\n85\t    |   |-- ui/              CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons/ (inline SVG\n86\t    |   |                    brand marks and UI glyphs)\n87\t    |   `-- motion-primitives/   vendored, patched locally, logged in an ADR\n88\t    |-- anim/\n89\t    |   |-- index.ts         public barrel: the only animation import path\n90\t    |   |-- MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx\n91\t    |   |-- useMotionPreference.ts\n92\t    |   |-- tokens.ts        springs, durations, eases in both libraries' formats\n93\t    |   |-- MotionFeatures.tsx   LazyMotion provider with the async feature chunk\n94\t    |   |-- declarative/     motion-owned wrappers (ToastPresence,\n95\t    |   |                    NavIndicator, PillMorph, TextReveal)\n96\t    |   `-- physics/         react-spring-owned wrappers (ThemeThumb and other\n97\t    |                        pointer-driven, velocity-carrying helpers)\n98\t    |-- content/\n99\t    |   |-- schema.ts        every type\n100\t    |   |-- profile.ts, experience.ts, projects.ts, skills.ts,\n101\t    |   |   education.ts, contacts.ts     one module per section\n102\t    |   |-- images.ts        image registry\n103\t    |   `-- index.ts         assembles siteContent with satisfies\n104\t    |-- hooks/               useActiveSection, useTheme\n105\t    |-- lib/                 cn.ts, date formatting, a11y helpers\n106\t    |-- styles/\n107\t    |   |-- index.css        tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping\n108\t    |   |-- tokens.css       role tokens, derived tokens, light and dark blocks\n109\t    |   `-- base.css         resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts\n110\t    `-- assets/images/       hashed by Vite\n111\t```\n112\t\n113\tThe animation folder is named anim/ rather than motion/ so it is never confused with the motion package or its motion/react import path.\n114\t\n115\t## Data flow\n116\t\n117\tBuild time:\n118\t\n119\t```text\n120\tcontent/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> app/Page --props--> sections --props--> layout, ui\n121\tassets/*     --(imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\n122\t```\n123\t\n124\tRuntime, all client-side with no network use beyond same-origin assets:\n125\t\n126\t- Theme: an inline script in the document head reads localStorage, falls back to prefers-color-scheme, and sets the data-theme attribute on the html element before first paint. The theme provider in React only changes the attribute and storage on a user toggle.\n127\t- Motion preference: the OS reduced-motion setting (plus an optional site override) feeds one hook, useMotionPreference, which configures both animation libraries and the wrapper components. See Reduced motion.\n128\t- Scroll-spy: an IntersectionObserver over the section ids drives useActiveSection, which highlights the current section in the navigation.\n129\t- Pointer and touch input drive react-spring values written straight to the DOM, with no React re-render per frame.\n130\t- Project detail: a click or tap sets local state in the Projects section, which opens an overlay with focus handling.\n131\t\n132\tThere is no global state library. Three small contexts exist: theme, motion preference and, optionally, the active section. Everything else is props.\n133\t\n134\t## Content model\n135\t\n136\tAll types live in src/content/schema.ts:\n137\t\n138\t- Profile: name, tagline, bio paragraphs, optional quote, photo (ImageAsset), contacts.\n139\t- ContactLink: kind (email, github, linkedin or instagram), label, href, whether it is external.\n140\t- ExperienceEntry: id, role, org, optional org URL, optional logo, dates (DateRange), location, bullets, optional highlights and stack.\n141\t- Project: id, title, context kind (personal, employer, course or hackathon) with a label, dates, stack, bullets, relatedExperienceId, featured, optional links and image.\n142\t- EducationEntry: id, degree, school, location, dates, gpa, optional track.\n143\t- SkillGroup: id, label, items as a string array.\n144\t- DateRange: ISO start and end (or present) plus a display label in one normalized format.\n145\t- ImageAsset: src, alt, width, height, optional srcset.\n146\t\n147\tRules:\n148\t\n149\t- Content modules use satisfies against the schema so a missing field fails typecheck.\n150\t- Ordering lives in the data (array order or a priority field), never in components.\n151\t- Copy is taken verbatim from the reviewed content inventory; any edit is recorded there with before and after, not made silently in code.\n152\t- The featured flag decides what appears first on mobile, so \"key info in seconds\" is a data decision.\n153\t- Content tests check that ids are unique, related ids resolve, every image has alt text, every external link is https, and dates parse in descending order.\n154\t- A phone number is not a field anywhere in the model, and must never appear in site copy, metadata or the served resume.\n155\t\n156\t## Design tokens\n157\t\n158\t- Exactly five role tokens per theme: text, background, primary, secondary, accent. Light and dark are authored separately, not inverted.\n159\t- Derived tokens (surface, border, muted text, link, focus ring and so on) are computed from the five roles with color-mix(in oklch, ...). A palette swap therefore edits one block and every derived value follows.\n160\t- The theme is selected by the data-theme attribute on the html element.\n161\t- Tailwind v4 is configured CSS-first: a custom dark variant is bound to [data-theme=dark] with @custom-variant, and an @theme inline block maps utilities to the CSS variables. There is no tailwind.config.js.\n162\t- No raw hex values in components. Components use semantic utilities only; a CI check greps for stray hex.\n163\t- A contrast test recomputes every text, UI and focus pair in both themes against WCAG 2.2 AA (4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text and UI) and fails the build on any miss.\n164\t- Spring and duration values that JavaScript needs are mirrored in CSS variables, with src/anim/tokens.ts as the canonical source for springs.\n165\t\n166\t## Animation layer\n167\t\n168\tsrc/anim/index.ts is the only animation import path for the rest of the app. It exposes a handful of semantic components and hooks, not library primitives.\n169\t\n170\t| Interaction class | Owner |\n171\t|-------------------|-------|\n172\t| Mount and unmount, presence, exit animations | motion |\n173\t| Layout and shared-element transitions | motion |\n174\t| Scroll-linked values and in-view reveals | motion |\n175\t| Text and number effects, stagger, ready-made motion-primitives prefabs | motion |\n176\t| Velocity-carrying physics: press-squish, rail hover lift with neighbor shift, theme toggle thumb | react-spring |\n177\t| Color and hover transitions, native scroll-snap rail swipe | CSS |\n178\t| Desktop wheel smoothing only | Lenis |\n179\t\n180\tThe rule: one library animates any given element. Never both libraries on one element or one property, and no Tailwind transition utilities on an element a library animates. If a vendored primitive duplicates a physics wrapper, one implementation is chosen per interaction and the other stays unused.\n181\t\n182\tSpring and duration tokens are defined once in src/anim/tokens.ts, exported in both libraries' formats (motion's stiffness and damping, react-spring's tension and friction), so the two feel like one system. Fine-pointer effects such as tilt, magnetic pull and spotlight are gated by (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine); touch gets a tap or press equivalent, or nothing. Every wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards className, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n183\t\n184\t## Reduced motion\n185\t\n186\tOne preference source, useMotionPreference, combines the OS prefers-reduced-motion setting with an optional site override and feeds three consumers:\n187\t\n188\t- motion: the provider renders MotionConfig with its reducedMotion setting. This disables transform and layout animation but not opacity or color, so wrappers handle parallax, scroll-linked and autoplay effects explicitly.\n189\t- react-spring: the provider calls Globals.assign({ skipAnimation }) so every spring jumps to its target; the effect cleans up on change.\n190\t- CSS: a @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) guard in base.css shortens transitions and animations and keeps scroll behavior native.\n191\t\n192\tContent is visible by default and is never hidden until JavaScript runs. Hidden initial states are applied client-side inside wrappers, and a noscript rule guarantees visibility. Pressed, focused and selected states must read without motion (color, outline, shape). Lenis is off under reduced motion. The provider listens for changes to the media query so a live OS toggle takes effect without a reload.\n193\t\n194\t## Assets\n195\t\n196\t- Images live in src/assets/images/ and are imported as modules (hashed, long-cached). vite-imagetools produces AVIF and WebP variants and srcset with intrinsic width and height to prevent layout shift. Below-the-fold images lazy-load; the hero image is high priority.\n197\t- Fonts are self-hosted through Fontsource (variable, latin subset, font-display swap), at most two families. No third-party font CDN.\n198\t- Brand icons (GitHub, LinkedIn, Instagram, email) and UI glyphs (close, chevrons, download) are inline SVG components under src/components/ui/icons/. No icon package is installed. See ADR-0005 and ADR-0010 in the ADR log.\n199\t- The resume PDF served from public/ is the owner's phone-number-free export of the resume. The phone-number gate reads its text layer, link annotations and metadata, and rejects a stand-in file.\n200\t- Only images that are actually used migrate from the previous site, with EXIF metadata stripped. Template leftovers are left behind.\n201\t\n202\t## Build and deploy\n203\t\n204\tGitHub Actions builds the site and publishes it to GitHub Pages. The Vite base comes from the VITE_BASE environment variable: the project URL path (for example /aa-phan-portfolio-v2/) while staging, and / after the cutover to the user-site URL. Assets are module-imported so no absolute asset paths exist; anything under public/ that JavaScript references goes through import.meta.env.BASE_URL. CI also builds with the project-path base so a broken sub-path is caught before launch. The build has a second HTML entry, 404.html, a static not-found page that GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths. Steps, settings, cutover and rollback are in the deploy runbook (deploy-runbook.md).\n205\t\n206\t## Quality gates\n207\t\n208\t| Gate | Command | Fails the build when |\n209\t|------|---------|----------------------|\n210\t| Typecheck | `tsc -b` | A content field is missing or a reference is wrong |\n211\t| Lint with import boundaries | oxlint, warnings denied | A section imports an animation library or content data |\n212\t| Format check | Prettier with Tailwind class sorting | A file is not formatted |\n213\t| Unit tests | Vitest: shell parity, theme logic, motion preference, gate negatives | Any test fails |\n214\t| Path-case check | `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`, run locally and on Linux CI | An import differs in case from the file on disk |\n215\t| Raw color check | `scripts/check-colors.mjs` | Source outside the token file and vendored primitives holds a raw hex, rgb, hsl, oklch or oklab value, or a Tailwind default palette utility |\n216\t| Token contrast test | `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` | Any text, UI or focus pair is below AA in any theme |\n217\t| Build | `vite build` | The Vite build errors |\n218\t| Initial JS budget | `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` | The module script and modulepreloads exceed 150 kB gzipped |\n219\t| Phone-number check | `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` over the built site | A served file holds a phone-shaped string or a tel: link, or a PDF's text layer, link annotations or metadata does (read with pdfjs-dist), or the PDF cannot be read, or resume.pdf is not a real resume (the owner's name and at least 1500 non-whitespace characters of text) |\n220\t| Image check | `scripts/check-images.mjs` over dist, public and src | A shipped raster is not AVIF or WebP, carries Exif or XMP, or is unreferenced, or a source image is not imported |\n221\t| Smoke tests | Playwright in CI | The hero is missing with JavaScript off, the theme flashes, or content is hidden or a transform animation runs with reduced motion emulated |\n222\t| axe | Later gate | Serious or critical violations in light or dark |\n223\t| Mobile Lighthouse | Later gate | Targets set in the scaffold milestone are missed |\n224\t\n225\t`npm run verify` chains the first eleven rows in that order, and both CI workflows call it.\n226\t\n227\t## Stack\n228\t\n229\tVerified 2026-10-08; re-verify at scaffold.\n230\t\n231\t| Technology | Version | Purpose |\n232\t|------------|---------|---------|\n233\t| Vite | ^8.3.4 | Build and dev server |\n234\t| React and React DOM | ^19.3.0 | UI |\n235\t| TypeScript | ~6.0.3 | Types |\n236\t| @vitejs/plugin-react | ^6.1.2 | React transform |\n237\t| Tailwind CSS and @tailwindcss/vite | ^4.3.3 | Styling, first-party Vite plugin |\n238\t| motion | ~14.0.0 | Declarative animation, imported from motion/react |\n239\t| @react-spring/web | ~10.1.2 | Spring physics |\n240\t| clsx | ^2.1.1 | Class composition |\n241\t| tailwind-merge | ^3.7.0 | Class conflict resolution |\n242\t| lenis | 1.3.x | Desktop wheel smoothing |\n243\t| vite-imagetools | ^12.0.1 | Image resize and format variants |\n244\t| pdfjs-dist | 6.3.289 (exact, dev only) | so there is no icon package. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WoSa13X8TUX4PfkRFa3wkE","type":"tool_result","content":"1\texport type Theme = 'light' | 'dark'\n2\t\n3\texport const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'theme'\n4\t\n5\t/** The current theme, read from html[data-theme]. Only 'dark' counts as dark. */\n6\texport function readTheme(): Theme {\n7\t  return document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n8\t}\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * True when the visitor chose a theme but storage refused it. Storage is the record of an\n12\t * explicit choice, so without this flag a blocked-storage visitor would look like one who\n13\t * never chose, and the next OS scheme change would silently undo their choice.\n14\t */\n15\tlet unsavedChoice = false\n16\t\n17\t/**\n18\t * Switch the theme: the attribute is written first so the switch always works, then the\n19\t * choice is stored. When storage is blocked the choice lasts for the page session only, and\n20\t * the OS scheme no longer overrides it.\n21\t */\n22\texport function applyTheme(theme: Theme): void {\n23\t  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n24\t  try {\n25\t    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, theme)\n26\t    unsavedChoice = false\n27\t  } catch {\n28\t    // Storage blocked (private mode, policy): the in-memory choice still applies.\n29\t    unsavedChoice = true\n30\t  }\n31\t}\n32\t\n33\tconst DARK_QUERY = '(prefers-color-scheme: dark)'\n34\t\n35\tfunction isTheme(value: unknown): value is Theme {\n36\t  return value === 'light' || value === 'dark'\n37\t}\n38\t\n39\tfunction storedTheme(): Theme | null {\n40\t  try {\n41\t    const value = localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)\n42\t    return isTheme(value) ? value : null\n43\t  } catch {\n44\t    return null\n45\t  }\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\tfunction systemTheme(): Theme {\n49\t  try {\n50\t    return window.matchMedia(DARK_QUERY).matches ? 'dark' : 'light'\n51\t  } catch {\n52\t    return 'light'\n53\t  }\n54\t}\n55\t\n56\t/** Write the attribute without storing anything, and only when it would change. */\n57\tfunction syncAttribute(theme: Theme): void {\n58\t  if (readTheme() !== theme || !document.documentElement.hasAttribute('data-theme')) {\n59\t    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n60\t  }\n61\t}\n62\t\n63\t/**\n64\t * Subscribe to theme changes made outside the toggle: another tab changing or clearing the\n65\t * stored choice, and the OS scheme changing while nothing is stored. Both are folded into\n66\t * html[data-theme], which stays the single source of truth, and `onChange` fires when that\n67\t * attribute changes for any reason. External contact is limited to outbound links in content data and the deployment pipeline on GitHub. There are no third-party SDKs, analytics or tracking scripts in `src/`.\n\n## APIs & External Services\n\n**Runtime APIs:**\n- None. No `fetch` or XHR calls in `src/`. The only `fetch` usages are in `tests/e2e/global-setup.ts` (readiness probe of the served site) and are test-only.\n\n**Outbound links (content, not API calls):**\n- GitHub profile, LinkedIn profile, Instagram profile - defined in `src/content/contacts.ts` (`kind: 'github' | 'linkedin' | 'instagram'`, `external: true`, all `https://`)\n- Project code link (GitHub repository) - `src/content/projects.ts` (`links.code`)\n- Email `mailto:` link - `src/content/contacts.ts` (`kind: 'email'`, `external: false`). The same address is also hard-coded as `mailto:` links in `index.html` (hero \"Copy email\" CTA). Keep both in sync when the address changes.\n\n**Brand icons:**\n- Inline SVG components for GitHub, LinkedIn and Instagram in `src/components/ui/icons/GitHubIcon.tsx`, `src/components/ui/icons/LinkedInIcon.tsx`, `src/components/ui/icons/InstagramIcon.tsx`. No icon CDN or package.\n\n**Fonts:**\n- Self-hosted through `@fontsource-variable/archivo` (npm). Imported in `src/main.tsx`. No Google Fonts or third-party font CDN, per `docs/architecture.md`.\n\n## Data Storage\n\n**Databases:**\n- Not applicable. No database client, ORM or connection string in the project.\n\n**File Storage:**\n- Local filesystem only, as static assets. Images in `src/assets/images/` are module-imported and hashed by Vite (`src/content/images.ts`). `public/resume.pdf` and `public/favicon.svg` are served as-is.\n\n**Caching:**\n- None on the server. Browser `localStorage` holds one key, `theme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`, `THEME_STORAGE_KEY`). The app tolerates blocked storage (see `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`).\n\n## Authentication & Identity\n\n**Auth Provider:**\n- None. The site has no login, sessions or user accounts.\n\n## Monitoring & Observability\n\n**Error Tracking:**\n- None detected. No Sentry or similar SDK in `package.json`.\n\n**Logs:**\n- None in the app. Build-gate scripts print to stdout/stderr in `scripts/*.mjs`.\n\n**Analytics:**\n- None detected.\n\n## CI/CD & Deployment\n\n**Hosting:**\n- GitHub Pages. Source and cutover are described in `docs/adr/0003-github-pages-source-and-cutover.md` and `docs/deploy-runbook.md`.\n\n**CI Pipeline:**\n- GitHub Actions, two workflows:\n  - `.github/workflows/ci.yml` - on `push` and `pull_request`. Runs `npm ci`, `npm run verify`, installs Chromium with `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium`, runs `npm run test:e2e`.\n  - `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` - on `push` to `main` and `workflow_dispatch`. Jobs: `build` (verify, then `actions/configure-pages` and `actions/upload-pages-artifact` from `./dist`), `e2e` (needs build), `deploy` (needs build and e2e, uses `actions/deploy-pages` and an OIDC token, then curls the published page and greps for the owner's name).\n- Actions are pinned by full commit SHA (see `uses:` lines in both workflows).\n- Required permissions: `contents: read` globally. `pages: write` and `id-token: write` only on the `deploy` job.\n\n**Dependency tooling:**\n- No Dependabot or Renovate config is present in the mapped tree.\n\n## Environment Configuration\n\n**Required env vars:**\n- None are required to run the site locally.\n- `VITE_BASE` - sets the Vite base path. Set in both workflows to the project path. Unset means `/`. Read in `vite.config.ts` and in `playwright.config.ts`.\n- `JS_BUDGET_BYTES` - optional, read in `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`.\n- `E2E_BASE_URL` - optional, read in `playwright.config.ts` to point E2E at a live URL.\n- `CI` - read by Playwright config.\n\n**Secrets location:**\n- No application secrets. The CI workflows use no repository secrets. Deploy authentication is OIDC through GitHub Pages (`id-token: write`), not a stored token.\n- Do not add secrets to `src/`, `index.html` or `public/`. Those are shipped to the browser.\n\n## Phone-number policy (build-time gate)\n\n- A phone number must never appear in site copy, metadata or the served resume. `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` scans the built `dist/` output, including `public/resume.pdf` text, link annotations and metadata via `pdfjs-dist`. It also rejects any `tel:` link. The phone number is not a field in the content schema (`src/content/schema.ts`).\n\n## Webhooks & Callbacks\n\n**Incoming:**\n- None.\n\n**Outgoing:**\n- None.\n\n---\n\n*Integration audit: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4136,"cache_read_input_tokens":64822,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4136,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01D7d4Ds4fJ7hgU2gEivuMh5","agent_id":"a9112bcfe36923424","session_id":"a0832ec3-4d55-4f99-9191-aa88a3f18ffb","uuid":"833e449a-8c31-4a4b-859c-bc02a3a039ee","timestamp":"2026-11-20T02:51:18.652Z","request_id":"req_011ChDRNpcSXBaYQrK5er1FE","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}

┌──────────────────────┐\n│ sections/         │   │ layout/              │   │ anim/ (only place    │\n│ `src/sections/*`  │   │ `src/layout/*`       │   │ motion + react-spring│\n│ props in, JSX out │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Static, client-only single-page React app with a build-time typed content layer. No router, no global store, no data fetching.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One route. `src/app/Page.tsx` renders every section in a fixed order. Section anchors (`#hero`, `#experience`, ...) drive navigation.\n- Props flow downward. Only three React contexts exist: theme (via `useSyncExternalStore` on `html[data-theme]`), motion preference, and toast.\n- Content is data, not components. Ordering is in the arrays in `src/content/*.ts`, and components do not hard-code copy.\n- Styling is CSS-variable driven. Five role colors per theme in `src/styles/tokens.css`, mapped into Tailwind v4 utilities in `src/styles/index.css`.\n- Architecture rules are enforced by lint (`.oxlintrc.json`), not by review alone.\n\n## Layers\n\n**app (composition root):**\n- Purpose: Mount the app, wire providers, compose the page.\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Depends on: `anim` barrel, `components/ui/Toast`, `content`, `hooks/useActiveSection`, `layout`, `sections`\n- Used by: Vite entry (`index.html` loads `/src/main.tsx`)\n\n**sections (feature UI):**\n- Purpose: One component per portfolio section, receiving data as props.\n- Location: `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/sections/Experience/Experience.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx` (with `Sleeve.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `project-types.ts`), `src/sections/About/About.tsx`, `src/sections/Skills/Skills.tsx`, `src/sections/Education/Education.tsx`, `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx`\n- Depends on: `layout`, `components/ui`, the `@/anim` barrel, content types (`@/content` types only)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**layout (chrome):**\n- Purpose: Page frame, navigation, header, footer, skip link, wordmark.\n- Location: `src/layout/` - `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`\n- Depends on: `anim` barrel, `lib`, `hooks`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, `src/sections/*`\n\n**components (shared UI):**\n- Purpose: Reusable primitives and vendored motion components.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/` (`CtaLink.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `icons/`), `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` (vendored, locally patched; see ADR-0007)\n- Depends on: `anim` barrel and `lib` (`motion-primitives` may import `motion/react`)\n- Used by: `layout`, `sections`, `app`\n\n**anim (animation layer):**\n- Purpose: The only code that imports `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web`. Exposes semantic wrappers, not library primitives.\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n  - `index.ts` - public barrel (the only allowed import path for other layers)\n  - `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts` - one reduced-motion source feeding `MotionConfig` (motion) and `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })` (react-spring)\n  - `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts`, `featuresStatus.ts` - `LazyMotion` boundary; the `domMax` feature bundle is a dynamic `import()` so it loads as a separate chunk\n  - `tokens.ts` - springs, durations and easing shared by both libraries\n  - `declarative/` - motion-owned wrappers: `ToastPresence.tsx`, `NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx`, `TextReveal.tsx`\n  - `physics/` - react-spring-owned wrappers: `ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `components/motion-primitives`, the token mirror in `styles`\n- Used by: everything above it through the barrel\n\n**hooks and lib (shared logic):**\n- Purpose: Stateful hooks and framework-neutral helpers.\n- Location: `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`), `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`)\n- `src/lib/theme.ts` is the one theme store; `useTheme` subscribes to it with `useSyncExternalStore`.\n- `useActiveSection` runs one `IntersectionObserver` over section ids to drive scroll-spy in `SiteHeader` and `PillNav`.\n\n**content (typed data):**\n- Purpose: All copy, links and dates.\n- Location: `src/content/` - `schema.ts` (every type), `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `index.ts` (assembles `siteContent`)\n- Depends on: `schema.ts`, `src/assets/images/` (through `images.ts`)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only. Enforced by oxlint rule `no-restricted-imports` (`**/content/**`).\n\n**styles and assets:**\n- `src/styles/tokens.css` - role tokens and derived tokens, light and dark blocks under `[data-theme]`\n- `src/styles/index.css` - Tailwind import, `@custom-variant dark`, `@theme inline` mapping\n- `src/styles/base.css` - resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, font setup\n- `src/assets/images/` - imported as modules. `about-dj.jpg`, `capitalone.png`, `spectrum.png`, `symbotic.png`\n\n## Import Rules\n\nDependencies point down only. Enforced by `.oxlintrc.json` (`no-restricted-imports`) and tested in `tests/unit/boundaries.test.ts`.\n\n| Importer | May import | May not import |\n|----------|-----------|----------------|\n| `src/sections/*` | `layout`, `components/ui`, `@/anim` barrel, content types | `motion/react`, `@react-spring/*`, `components/motion-primitives/*`, content data |\n| `src/layout/*`, `src/components/ui/*` | `@/anim` barrel, `lib`, `hooks` | `sections`, content data |\n| `src/components/motion-primitives/*` | `motion/react`, `lib/cn` | everything else |\n| `src/anim/*` | `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `motion-primitives`, token mirror in styles | `sections`, `layout`, `content` |\n| `src/app/Page.tsx` | content data, `sections`, `layout`, `hooks` | any animation library or anim internals |\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Build time\n\n```text\nsrc/content/*.ts  --(typed with satisfies schema.ts)-->  src/content/index.ts (siteContent)\n        |\n        v\nsrc/app/Page.tsx  --props-->  src/sections/*  --props-->  src/layout/*, src/components/ui/*\nsrc/assets/images/*  --(vite-imagetools, ?format=avif;webp&as=picture)-->  images.ts  -->  ImageAsset fields\n```\n\n### Primary request path (page load)\n\n1. Browser loads `index.html`. The inline script in `<head>` sets `data-theme` on `<html>` from `localStorage` key `theme`, falling back to `prefers-color-scheme` (`index.html`).\n2. `src/main.tsx` imports `@fontsource-variable/archivo/wdth.css` and `src/styles/index.css`, then calls `createRoot(#root).render(<StrictMode><App/></StrictMode>)`.\n3. `src/app/App.tsx` renders `Providers` around `Page`.\n4. `src/app/Providers.tsx` wraps children in `MotionPreferenceProvider` > `MotionFeatures` (`LazyMotion`, async `domMax` chunk) > `ToastProvider`.\n5. `src/app/Page.tsx` reads `siteContent`, derives the `email` from the `kind: 'email'` contact, and filters the remaining links for Contact.\n6. `Page.tsx` calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` once and passes `activeId` and `lockTo` to `SiteHeader` and `PillNav`.\n7. Sections render in fixed DOM order: `Hero`, `Experience`, `Projects`, `About`, `Skills`, `Education`, `Contact`. Then `Footer` and `PillNav`.\n\n### Secondary flows\n\n**Theme toggle:**\n1. `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` calls `useTheme().toggle` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n2. `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` writes `data-theme` on `<html>`, then stores the choice in `localStorage`. Storage failures are tolerated.\n3. `subscribeTheme` (MutationObserver, `storage` event, `matchMedia` change) keeps all subscribers in sync. The `ThemeThumb` physics wrapper (`src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`) animates the toggle.\n\n**Project detail:**\n1. A click or tap on a project in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx` or `Sleeve.tsx` sets local state.\n2. `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` renders a native `<dialog>` element (ADR-0006, ADR-0009) with focus handling.\n\n**Toast:**\n1. `useToast` (`src/components/ui/useToast.ts`) pushes entries into `ToastProvider` (`src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`).\n2. Exit and enter animations run through `ToastPresence` (`src/anim/declarative/ToastPresence.tsx`).\n\n**Copy email:**\n- `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` provides the copy action used by the hero and contact CTAs.\n\n**Scroll-spy:**\n- `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` runs a single `IntersectionObserver` over a band below the nav. It returns `activeId` (null when unsupported) and `lockTo` to pin the active id after a nav click.\n\n### State Management\n\n- Theme: the DOM attribute `html[data-theme]` is the source of truth. `useSyncExternalStore` reads it.\n- Motion preference: `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` combines `prefers-reduced-motion` with an optional site override. It feeds motion's `MotionConfig` and react-spring's `Globals`.\n- Active section: `useActiveSection` state, owned by `Page.tsx`.\n- Project dialog open state: local state in the Projects section.\n- Toasts: `ToastProvider` context.\n- No global store library, no server state.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**`siteContent` and the content schema:**\n- Purpose: Single typed source for all copy.\n- Examples: `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts`\n- Pattern: Each module exports a `satisfies`-checked array or object. Missing fields fail `tsc -b`.\n\n**`ImageAsset`:**\n- Purpose: Responsive image shape with `src`, `alt`, `width`, `height`, optional `sources` (`avif`, `webp`).\n- Examples: `src/content/images.ts` (`aboutPhoto`, employer logos), `src/content/schema.ts`\n- Pattern: Empty `alt` marks decorative images (employer logos).\n\n**Animation wrappers (`@/anim` barrel):**\n- Purpose: Stable semantic API so sections never touch the libraries. `src/anim/index.ts` exports `ThemeThumb`, `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `useMotionPreference`, `TextReveal`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`, `MotionFeatures`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`.\n- Pattern: Each wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards `className`, and never hides content in its initial state.\n\n**Navigation model:**\n- Purpose: Single list of sections and nav labels.\n- Examples: `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`, `NAV_ITEMS`)\n- Pattern: The hero has an anchor but no nav entry. `NAV_ITEMS` uses `satisfies NavItem[]`.\n\n**Design tokens:**\n- Purpose: One palette swap edits one block.\n- Examples: `src/styles/tokens.css`, `src/styles/index.css`\n- Pattern: Five role tokens per theme (text, background, primary, secondary, accent). Derived tokens use `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`. Components use semantic utilities, never raw hex.\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser entry:**\n- Location: `index.html` (main, with `<script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\">`)\n- Triggers: Page load of `/` (or `VITE_BASE`)\n- Responsibilities: Pre-paint theme init, base markup for the noscript case\n\n**Not-found entry:**\n- Location: `404.html` (second Vite input in `vite.config.ts`)\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serving an unknown path\n- Responsibilities: Static not-found page. Styled by `src/styles/not-found.css`.\n\n**React root:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Module evaluation\n- Responsibilities: Font import, global CSS import, `createRoot` into `#root`\n\n**Dev and build entries:**\n- `vite.config.ts` (dev server, build, `base` from `VITE_BASE`)\n- `tsconfig.app.json` (typecheck of `src/`)\n\n**Build gate scripts (not runtime):**\n- `scripts/check-*.mjs` run from `npm run verify`. Each inspects `src/`, `public/` or `dist/`.\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers or Service Workers in the mapped tree.\n- **Global state:** Module-level mutable state exists in `src/lib/theme.ts` (`unsavedChoice` flag) and `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts` (features-loaded marker). Other state is per-component.\n- **Circular imports:** None documented. Dependencies are enforced to point downward by lint.\n- **Base path:** All URLs under `public/` must go through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` (see `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx`). A hard-coded `/` breaks the project-path deploy.\n- **Content visibility:** Content is visible by default and never hidden until JS runs. The noscript rule in `src/styles/base.css` guarantees visibility.\n- **Reduced motion:** Respected in three places: `MotionConfig` (motion), `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })` (react-spring), and the CSS guard in `src/styles/base.css`.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing animation libraries outside `src/anim/`\n\n**What happens:** A section or layout file imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly, or imports deep paths like `@/anim/declarative/PillMorph`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks the one-library-per-element rule and the `@/anim` barrel contract. Lint fails the build (`.oxlintrc.json`).\n**Do this instead:** Import the semantic wrapper from the barrel: `import { PillMorph } from '@/anim'`, as `src/layout/PillNav.tsx` and the section files do.\n\n### Importing content data outside `Page.tsx`\n\n**What happens:** A section imports `@/content` or `@/content/projects` for copy.\n**Why it's wrong:** Violates the import rule. Lint fails. Sections stop being testable with props.\n**Do this instead:** Add the field to `src/content/*.ts` and `schema.ts`, then pass it from `src/app/Page.tsx` as a prop. Type-only imports from `@/content` (`ImageAsset`, `Project`, ...) are allowed.\n\n### Hard-coded public asset paths\n\n**What happens:** `href=\"/resume.pdf\"` or `src=\"/favicon.svg\"` in components.\n**Why it's wrong:** The project-path deploy (`VITE_BASE=/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/`) breaks.\n**Do this instead:** `` href={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}resume.pdf`} `` as in `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`.\n\n### Raw colors in components\n\n**What happens:** A component uses hex, rgb, `oklch()`, or a Tailwind default palette utility such as `bg-blue-500`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks the token system and fails `scripts/check-colors.mjs`.\n**Do this instead:** Use semantic utilities (`bg-background`, `text-text`, `text-muted`, `border-border-strong`) that map to role tokens in `src/styles/tokens.css`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Fail-soft at runtime. Motion and storage failures degrade to static or in-memory behavior and never block content.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- Lazy motion chunk failures return a never-settling promise, so `m` components stay static (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`).\n- `localStorage` access is wrapped in try/catch in `src/lib/theme.ts`. The in-memory choice still applies.\n- `matchMedia` absence is caught. OS scheme changes are then not followed.\n- `useActiveSection` returns `activeId: null` when `IntersectionObserver` is missing.\n- Build-time failures are hard failures: `tsc -b`, oxlint `--deny-warnings`, and the `check:*` scripts exit non-zero.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in the app. `console` is not used for runtime logging in `src/`.\n**Validation:** Compile-time via `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts`. Runtime validation is not needed (static data). Content invariants (unique ids, resolvable related ids, https external links, alt text, descending dates) are checked by `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), focus management in the project dialog, WCAG 2.2 AA contrast checked by `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` over `scripts/contrast-pairs.json`.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2590,"cache_read_input_tokens":68958,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2590,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01D7d4Ds4fJ7hgU2gEivuMh5","agent_id":"a9112bcfe36923424","session_id":"a0832ec3-4d55-4f99-9191-aa88a3f18ffb","uuid":"fd454196-1173-45d5-a323-569a0cd7d1d1","timestamp":"2026-11-20T02:51:49.867Z","request_id":"req_011ChDRPd6g56vCjg44GXLNZ","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}

ci.yml (verify + e2e on push/PR), deploy.yml (GitHub Pages)\n├── .avarice/              # local tool config (config.json)\n├── .claude/               # CLAUDE.md for agent instructions\n├── .planning/             # GSD planning docs: PROJECT, REQUIREMENTS, ROADMAP, STATE, phases/, codebase/\n├── docs/                  # architecture.md, deploy-runbook.md, contrast-matrix.md, adr/ (ADR log)\n├── public/                # URL-stable static files: favicon.svg, resume.pdf\n├── scripts/               # build-gate scripts (check-*.mjs), QA screenshot helpers, lib/\n├── src/                   # application source (see below)\n├── tests/                 # unit/ (Vitest), e2e/ (Playwright), setup.ts\n├── index.html             # main Vite entry\n├── 404.html               # second Vite entry (GitHub Pages not-found)\n├── vite.config.ts         # Vite build and dev config\n├── vitest.config.ts       # unit test config\n├── playwright.config.ts   # E2E config\n├── tsconfig.json          # solution file (references app, node, scripts, test configs)\n├── tsconfig.app.json      # typecheck for src/\n├── tsconfig.node.json     # per-phase PLAN.md, SUMMARY.md, CONTEXT.md, REVIEW.md, UAT.md\n```\n\nSources of truth for file names in this document: `docs/architecture.md` (folder structure section) and the tree listing in this repo.\n\n## Directory Purposes\n\n**`src/app/`:**\n- Purpose: Composition root.\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Key files: `src/app/Page.tsx` (only file that imports `@/content` data and `@/sections/*`)\n\n**`src/sections/`:**\n- Purpose: One folder per portfolio section. Each receives data as props.\n- Contains: `Hero/Hero.tsx`, `Experience/Experience.tsx`, `Projects/` (`Projects.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `project-types.ts`), `About/About.tsx`, `Skills/Skills.tsx`, `Education/Education.tsx`, `Contact/Contact.tsx`\n- Key files: `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`\n\n**`src/layout/`:**\n- Purpose: Page chrome and navigation.\n- Contains: `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`\n- Key files: `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (section ids and nav labels), `src/layout/PillNav.tsx` (phone nav)\n\n**`src/components/`:**\n- Purpose: Shared UI.\n- Contains:\n  - `ui/` - `CtaLink.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `icons/` (inline SVG: `ChevronLeftIcon.tsx`, `ChevronRightIcon.tsx`, `CloseIcon.tsx`, `DownloadIcon.tsx`, `GitHubIcon.tsx`, `InstagramIcon.tsx`, `LinkedInIcon.tsx`)\n  - `motion-primitives/` - `text-effect.tsx` (vendored, patched locally; logged in an ADR)\n\n**`src/anim/`:**\n- Purpose: The only animation import path. Holds the `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web` imports.\n- Contains:\n  - `index.ts` - barrel. Other folders import from `@/anim` only.\n  - `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts`, `featuresStatus.ts`, `tokens.ts`\n  - `declarative/` - `ToastPresence.tsx`, `NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx`, `TextReveal.tsx`\n  - `physics/` - `ThemeThumb.tsx`\n\n**`src/content/`:**\n- Purpose: Typed site copy and data. The only place copy lives.\n- Contains: `schema.ts`, `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `index.ts`\n\n**`src/hooks/`:**\n- Purpose: React hooks. `useActiveSection.ts` (scroll-spy), `useTheme.ts` (theme store subscription)\n\n**`src/lib/`:**\n- Purpose: Framework-neutral helpers. `theme.ts` (theme store and storage), `copy.ts`, `utils.ts` (`cn`)\n\n**`src/styles/`:**\n- Purpose: CSS. Tailwind v4 CSS-first config plus design tokens.\n- Contains: `index.css` (Tailwind import, `@custom-variant dark`, `@theme inline`), `tokens.css` (role and derived tokens, light and dark), `base.css` (resets, focus, reduced-motion guard, fonts, noscript rule), `interactions.css` (interaction states), `not-found.css` (404 page)\n\n**`src/assets/images/`:**\n- Purpose: Raster source images imported as modules. `about-dj.jpg`, `capitalone.png`, `spectrum.png`, `symbotic.png`.\n- Generated: Variants (AVIF, WebP) are generated at build time by `vite-imagetools`. Not committed.\n\n**`src/types/`:**\n- Purpose: Ambient type declarations. `imagetools.d.ts` declares the `?w=...&as=picture` import query types.\n\n**`public/`:**\n- Purpose: Files served at a stable URL. Referenced through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`.\n- Contains: `favicon.svg`, `resume.pdf`\n\n**`tests/unit/`:**\n- Purpose: Vitest unit tests. Covers content invariants, theme logic, motion preference, sections, layout, tokens, boundaries, gates.\n- Key files: `tests/unit/boundaries.test.ts` (import rules), `tests/unit/content.test.ts`, `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`, `tests/unit/theme.test.ts`, `tests/unit/gates.test.ts`, `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx`\n- Setup: `tests/setup.ts`\n\n**`tests/e2e/`:**\n- Purpose: Playwright specs run against the built site.\n- Contains: `smoke.spec.ts`, `hero.spec.ts`, `nav.spec.ts`, `theme.spec.ts`, `contact.spec.ts`, `content.spec.ts`, `copy-email.spec.ts`, `project-dialog.spec.ts`, `resume.spec.ts`, `not-found.spec.ts`, `wordmark.spec.ts`, `global-setup.ts`\n- Subfolder: `tests/e2e/mobile/` - `pill.spec.ts` (Pixel 7 project only)\n\n**`scripts/`:**\n- Purpose: Build gates run by `npm run verify`, plus QA helpers.\n- Contains: `check-colors.mjs`, `check-contrast.mjs`, `check-images.mjs`, `check-js-budget.mjs`, `check-no-phone.mjs`, `check-path-case.mjs`, `check-qa-shots.mjs`, `qa-extra-shots.mjs`, `contrast-pairs.json`, `lib/color.mjs`, `lib/tokens.mjs`\n\n**`docs/`:**\n- Purpose: Human documentation. `architecture.md` (source of the layer rules), `deploy-runbook.md`, `contrast-matrix.md`, `adr/` (`0001` to `0010` plus `README.md`).\n\n**`.planning/`:**\n- Purpose: Project planning state for the GSD workflow. Not shipped.\n- Contains: `PROJECT.md`, `REQUIREMENTS.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, `STATE.md`, `WINDOWS.md`, `phases/01-decisions-and-documentation/`, `phases/02-deployed-themed-skeleton/`, `phases/03-content-complete-site/`, and later phases, plus `codebase/` (these maps).\n\n## Key File Locations\n\n**Entry Points:**\n- `index.html`: main HTML entry with inline theme-init script\n- `404.html`: not-found entry\n- `src/main.tsx`: React root\n- `src/app/App.tsx`: app shell\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: build and dev\n- `tsconfig.app.json`: `@/*` path alias and strict settings\n- `.oxlintrc.json`: lint and import boundaries\n- `playwright.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`\n- `package.json`: scripts and dependency versions\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/app/Page.tsx`: section order and content wiring\n- `src/anim/index.ts`: animation barrel\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: theme store\n- `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`: scroll-spy\n- `src/content/index.ts`: `siteContent`\n- `src/content/schema.ts`: content types\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`, `tests/e2e/`, `tests/setup.ts`\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`. Example: `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/ToastPresence.tsx`\n- Hooks: camelCase starting with `use`, `.ts` or `.tsx`. Example: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`\n- Non-component modules: camelCase. Example: `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`, `src/lib/utils.ts`, `src/content/profile.ts`\n- Layout and nav helper modules: kebab-case. Example: `src/layout/nav-items.ts`, `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`\n- Section folders: PascalCase directory with a component file of the same name. Example: `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`\n- Vendored or upstream-style files: kebab-case. Example: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`\n- Tests: `*.test.ts` or `*.test.tsx` in `tests/unit/`. E2E: `*.spec.ts` in `tests/e2e/`. Scripts: `check-*.mjs`.\n- Assets: lowercase with hyphens or plain names. Example: `src/assets/images/about-dj.jpg`, `public/favicon.svg`\n\n**Directories:**\n- Feature folders under `src/sections/`: PascalCase (`Hero`, `Projects`)\n- Infrastructure folders under `src/`: lowercase (`app`, `anim`, `lib`, `hooks`, `styles`, `content`, `layout`)\n- Subfolders by role inside `src/anim/`: `declarative/` (motion-owned), `physics/` (react-spring-owned)\n\n**Path alias:**\n- `@/` maps to `src/` (`vite.config.ts`, `tsconfig.app.json`, `vitest.config.ts`). Always use `@/...` for `src` imports. The one exception is relative imports inside `src/anim/` (for example `./features`).\n\n**Path case:** Import paths must match on-disk case exactly. `scripts/check-path-case.mjs` enforces this for Linux CI.\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New section:**\n- Component: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`\n- Data: `src/content/<name>.ts` plus a type in `src/content/schema.ts`, then assembled in `src/content/index.ts`\n- Wiring: add the component and its data prop in `src/app/Page.tsx` in the order it should appear\n- Nav entry (if it has one): `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS` and `NAV_ITEMS`)\n- Tests: `tests/unit/sections.test.tsx` (component), `tests/unit/content.test.ts` (data invariants), `tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts` (smoke)\n\n**New shared UI component:**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`\n- Icon: `src/components/ui/icons/<Name>Icon.tsx` as inline SVG. Do not add an icon package (ADR-0010).\n\n**New animation effect:**\n- Declarative (mount, exit, layout, in-view): `src/anim/declarative/<Name>.tsx`, using `motion/react`\n- Physics (velocity, pointer-driven): `src/anim/physics/<Name>.tsx`, using `@react-spring/web`\n- Export from `src/anim/index.ts`. Do not import the library from anywhere else.\n- Reduced-motion: read `useMotionPreference` and render the instant equivalent.\n\n**New hook:** `src/hooks/use<Name>.ts`\n\n**New helper (framework-neutral):** `src/lib/<name>.ts`. Class composition goes in `src/lib/utils.ts` (`cn`).\n\n**New content field:** Add to `src/content/schema.ts` first, then fill it in the matching `src/content/*.ts` module. `satisfies` will fail typecheck for any module that is missing it.\n\n**New image:** Put the source in `src/assets/images/`, add the import with `?w=...&format=avif;webp&as=picture` in `src/content/images.ts`, and expose an `ImageAsset`. Images must be AVIF or WebP in the build and carry no EXIF or XMP (`scripts/check-images.mjs`).\n\n**New static file served by URL:** `public/<file>`, referenced through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`.\n\n**New design token:** Add to the role or derived block in `src/styles/tokens.css` (both light and dark). Expose it in `src/styles/index.css` `@theme inline` if a utility is needed. Do not add raw color values to components.\n\n**New build gate or QA script:** `scripts/check-<name>.mjs`, then add to the `verify` chain in `package.json`.\n\n**New ADR:** `docs/adr/00NN-<slug>.md` and an entry in `docs/adr/README.md`.\n\n## Special Directories\n\n**`dist/`:**\n- Purpose: Vite build output, uploaded to GitHub Pages\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No (`.gitignore`)\n\n**`node_modules/`:**\n- Purpose: npm dependencies\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No (`.gitignore`)\n\n**`node_modules/.tmp/`:**\n- Purpose: TypeScript build info (`tsBuildInfoFile`
